SECTION 1: BIOGRAPHY: BETSY DUFF, COO

A Managing Partner with the firm, Betsy has over 25 years of experience in private and investment banking and corporate finance. She is the firm's Chief Operating Officer. From 2001 to 2011, Betsy served as a Private Client Advisor at U.S. Trust, Bank of America Private Wealth Management. Prior to joining U.S. Trust, Betsy worked in Banc of America Securities' Global Project Finance unit providing risk mitigation and debt restructuring services in emerging markets, and in Global Corporate Finance arranging debt products for large corporate clients. She earned a B.A. from Brown University. Betsy has served as a Trustee and Chair of the Board of The Potomac School, Director of the Wolf Trap Foundation for the Performing Arts, and Board Member and President of the Junior League of Washington. She currently serves on the Board of Directors of The Literacy Lab.

### 2. [Foundry Robotics raises $100 million to build 'America's first autonomous factory for assembly'](https://ct.moreover.com/?a=60747165910&p=21l&v=1&x=ZMaM3YMb48pruaESO5BDlQ)
- **Outlet(s):** Newstex Blogs Robotics and Automation News | **Published:** `2026-10-31` | **Sentiment:** `POSITIVE` | **Positioning Relevance Score:** `1.00`
- **Matched Innodata Pillars:** Robotic Engineering, Agent Engineering, Infrastructure Engineering
- **Matched Competitors:** Scale AI, Turing
- **Coverage Snippet:** October 31st, 2026 ( Robotics and Automation News - Delivered by Newstex )

Foundry Robotics has announced its oversubscribed $100 million Series A funding round led by Marlinspike Partners.

Participating in the round are early backers Khosla Ventures and Red Glass Ventures, and new investors SE Ventures, Wing VC, and Axiom Ventures, as well as existing investors Hanabi and Zero Shot Fund.

Foundry builds and operates autonomous factories for complex assembly, combining AI-powered robotic cells with experienced manufacturing experts.

With the new funding, Foundry will build out its Emeryville facility, accelerate deployment of AI models into real-world manufacturing, and grow its robotics research, engineering, and factory operations teams.

Foundry has expanded its manufacturing footprint twentyfold to a 130,000-square-foot facility in Emeryville. The company currently serves defense and commercial industries, focusing on high-value, complex electro-mechanical products where production is constrained.

Applications include drones, autonomous vehicle subassemblies, wire harnesses, underwater vehicles, telecom/data center infrastructure, ground robots, rugged computers, robot compute boxes, satellites, and more.

Foundry has also expanded its multidisciplinary team, bringing together robotics researchers, AI and software engineers, and experienced manufacturing experts to develop, deploy, and continuously improve its technology directly on the factory floor.

'We believe the factories that revive America's manufacturing sector will need to be rethought from the ground up rather than retrofitting one aspect in an existing factory,' said Adarsh Kulkarni, founder and CEO of Foundry Robotics.

'Our approach is to own the entire final assembly stack and integrate intelligence at every layer - own the buildings that manufacture for our customers while orienting them for robotics deployment. Our mission is to revitalize US manufacturing and create more capacity to build and scale critical product assembly.'

Defense and industrial companies can design new hardware faster than ever, but manufacturing remains a critical bottleneck. Traditional assembly lines are built around a single product, take months to deploy, and are costly to reconfigure.

Foundry takes a different approach, leveraging physical AI to build flexible assembly capacity that deploys quickly, adapts to different products, and scales with demand.

'Production capacity is a national constraint on getting unmanned systems to the fleet,' said Duane Fotheringham, president, HII Unmanned Systems. 'Foundry's approach to autonomous assembly is exactly the kind of capability the defense industrial base needs right now.'

Through its manufacturing-as-a-service model, Foundry owns and operates the factory, taking customers from design to production in weeks without new production line investments or expensive retooling.

Foundry trains AI models on the building blocks of assembly and deploys these models in a grid of robotic 'cells' with manufacturing-grade precision. FoundryOS serves as a single agentic system linking the robots, the factory floor and the cloud.

Every production run feeds back into the models, so the same cell that assembles a drone today can learn to build a satellite component tomorrow, and gets better at both with every run.

'The next era of industrial strength will belong to nations that can turn new designs into finished hardware the fastest,' said Mislav Tolusic, managing partner at Marlinspike.

'Defense and industrial equipment manufacturers urgently need more domestic assembly capacity, and Foundry is delivering. Their AI-powered factory offers scalable and flexible assembly capacity.

'We are impressed with the speed that Adarsh and Emmanuel are executing - assembling an impressive team, commissioning the first factory, and quickly signing customers.'

Adarsh Kulkarni co-founded Foundry with Emmanuel Lamacchia, the former program manager for the Tesla Model Y program. Adarsh brings robotics and defense industry experience, as employee number 4 at Ghost Robotics, where he built legged robots for the US military, and later was Scale AI's inaugural Head of Robotics.

He built partnerships with companies like Generalist AI and Physical Intelligence while ushering in the modern third-party robotics data industry.

Emmanuel was the single-threaded owner for Tesla's Model Y, the best selling car in the world - responsible for manufacturing approximately 1.5 million cars a year.

The company's leadership team also includes former Tesla software leader Anup Karmakar and former Marine, Army, and Air Force veterans who worked at Raytheon and Northrop Grumman.

Link to the original story.

September 22 2026: Innodata's Future Tied to Meta's Muse Success

Meta's $200 billion bet on AI has significantly transformed Innodata Inc., a New Jersey-based data company. On 22nd September 2026, hiring records and insights from former employees reveal a close connection between Meta and Innodata, particularly with the recent success of Meta's AI tool, Muse, which has topped the Apple app store charts. Amid concerns that Meta may shift its workload away from Innodata following its investment in Scale AI, a new contract focusing on the personalization of long-horizon agents indicates a continuing partnership.

Innodata, which has historically worked with Meta, had approximately 400 full-time equivalent employees dedicated to the Meta account in 2024. Despite recent layoffs and reports of declining business with Meta, the announcement of a new initiative centered on personalization hints at a critical role for Innodata in developing Muse, although neither company has confirmed the connection. The specifics around the scale and financial implications of this contract remain undisclosed, leading to speculation about Innodata's future prospects.

As AI continues to evolve, the demand for high-quality data has surged, prompting companies like Innodata to adapt their offerings. The intricate relationship between compute power and data quality underpins the current landscape, making data a vital asset for AI models. Although many AI firms have already harvested vast amounts of readily available data, the need for specialized, high-quality datasets is growing. This trend has positioned Innodata as a key player, especially with its focus on providing training material and human judgment for complex AI tasks.

Muse's recent success—achieving the number one spot on Apple's U.S. free iPhone app chart—has fueled interest in Innodata's involvement. Meta's $14.3 billion stake in Scale AI has raised questions about potential in-house shifts, yet former employees assert that Meta's partnership with Innodata is likely to deepen rather than diminish. Innodata's CEO, Jack Abuhoff, has emphasized the company's commitment to growing alongside its largest customer and diversifying its client base, which now includes a second unnamed major tech player.

Despite facing challenges, including scrutiny from short sellers and executive share sales, Innodata remains optimistic about its future. The company has begun to pivot towards new opportunities in AI, showcasing its innovative approaches to training AI models. As Meta's Muse continues to gain traction, the underlying relationship between Innodata and Meta may prove to be a significant asset, allowing Innodata to benefit from the ongoing demand for advanced AI training and expertise.

Recent News 2 of 2, Source: HourlyBusinessNews

September 22 2026: Innodata Inc. Stock Surges as Hunterbrook Links Company to Meta's Muse

Shares of Innodata Inc. (INOD) jumped nearly 15% on Tuesday, marking its best day since mid-May, after a Hunterbrook research note indicated that the data company may be assisting in the training of Meta's new Muse assistant. The announcement, made on the 23rd of September 2026 at 16:45:29, saw INOD close at $69.97, up $8.92, after trading as high as $74.93. This positive momentum continued with shares edging up another 2% in after-hours trading.

Hunterbrook Media’s affiliate, Hunterbrook Capital, disclosed that it holds a long position in Innodata, further fueling investor interest. The research firm pointed out that former employees and current job listings suggest Meta is Innodata's largest customer, with estimates placing that account at approximately $146 million, accounting for 58% of the company's projected sales for 2025. The firm noted that Innodata recently described its work for Meta as focusing on the 'personalization of long-horizon agents,' which aligns with the capabilities of Muse.

Meta launched Muse on September 8, 2026, positioning it as a consumer agent capable of browsing the web, managing emails and calendars, and executing various tasks. Within just ten days, Muse achieved a top ranking among free iPhone applications in the United States, boasting a 4.9-star rating. Early reports indicated that downloads reached millions, with Muse briefly surpassing ChatGPT in popularity on Apple's U.S. free app chart.

Innodata specializes in providing training data and human review services to AI developers. Following a June high of approximately $121.50, the stock had faced downward pressure amid concerns that Meta might consolidate its work in-house after acquiring a significant stake in Scale AI. However, Hunterbrook reported insights from a former Innodata employee suggesting that the opposite trend has emerged, reinforcing the company's critical role in Meta's projects.

Innodata's second-quarter results revealed a shift in customer concentration, with the top customer contributing 37% of revenue, down from 56% in the first quarter, while another major tech client increased its share to 34%. The company reported quarterly sales of $92.1 million, reflecting a 58% increase from the previous year. Following the Hunterbrook report, retail sentiment surrounding Innodata stock has shifted from 'bullish' to 'extremely bullish,' with increased message volume indicating heightened investor interest.

SECTION 4 CORPORATE PROFILE

4.1 Activities

Innodata Inc., a Nasdaq-listed company, is a global leader in AI-enabled data engineering services, specializing in transforming unstructured data into actionable insights for clients across technology, healthcare, finance, and other sectors. The company's core activities encompass data annotation, content creation, AI model training, and digital operations, enabling businesses to leverage machine learning for improved efficiency and innovation. Its fastest-growing segments include generative AI solutions and AI training data services, fueled by surging demand for advanced analytics and automation in response to digital transformation trends. With a strong emphasis on scalability and quality, Innodata delivers tailored solutions that help organizations harness big data, maintain compliance, and drive competitive advantages in an increasingly data-driven economy. It is the NASDAQ's 14th largest Business services company by market capitalization.

Oct 30, 2026: ExlService Holdings EXL named an OpenAI Select Partner

NEW YORK, Oct. 29, 2026 -- EXL [NASDAQ: EXLS], a global data and AI company, announced that it has been named an OpenAI Select Partner within the OpenAI Partner Network.

The OpenAI Partner Network is a global program that equips partners with the resources, enablement, and support to build, sell, and deliver AI solutions with OpenAI. It brings together partners with deep industry expertise, delivery capabilities, and customer relationships while equipping them with resources, enablement, and support to help enterprises adopt OpenAI frontier models and products and turn them into measurable impact.

As an OpenAI Select Partner, EXL will work with OpenAI to help organizations build, deploy, and scale AI responsibly. This work will help organizations put OpenAI frontier models to work across their business, using deeper reasoning and computer use to complete workflows and deliver finished work.

"Being named an OpenAI Select Partner reflects how we work with clients: combining frontier AI with deep understanding of their business to deliver outcomes," said Vishal Chhibbar, executive vice president and chief revenue officer, EXL. "Clients trust us to apply that capability where it matters most, across entire journeys and with accountability for the end result. That trust, built over years inside their operations is what turns AI ambition into results."

EXL brings together data, context and AI to help organizations in insurance, financial services, healthcare and life sciences, and other heavily regulated, operationally complex industries move AI from pilot to production in core enterprises. Its work includes helping insurers reimagine underwriting, claims management and administrative workflows; supporting health plans integration of AI payment integrity and patient engagement systems; and transforming risk and compliance, credit decisioning and customer engagement initiatives for financial service leaders.

Q3 Adjusted Diluted EPS (Non-GAAP) (1) of $0.62, up 28.5% from $0.48 in Q3 of 2025

NEW YORK, Oct. 27, 2026 -- ExlService Holdings, Inc. (NASDAQ: EXLS), a global data and AI company, today announced its financial results for the quarter ended September 30, 2026.

Chairman and Chief Executive Officer Rohit Kapoor said, "We entered 2026 with strong momentum that has accelerated through the year, delivering third quarter revenue growth of 20% year-on-year and adjusted diluted EPS growth of 29% year-on-year. Every segment of our business grew more than 15% organically. Data and AI-led revenue, almost two-thirds of our business, grew 36% on a reported basis and 31% organically. These results reflect the strength of our data and AI strategy and our differentiated market position: deep domain expertise combined with data and AI capabilities that help clients make AI work effectively across the enterprise. Our business model affords us very good visibility into the balance of the year, and we look forward to a solid finish to 2026."

Chief Financial Officer Maurizio Nicolelli said, "Our strong third quarter performance gives us the confidence to raise our full-year outlook once again. We now expect full-year 2026 total revenue to be in the range of $2.430 billion to $2.440 billion, up from our prior guidance of $2.390 billion to $2.415 billion, reflecting 16% to 17% year-over-year growth on a reported basis and 15% on an organic constant currency basis. We now expect adjusted diluted earnings per share of $2.34 to $2.36, a 20% to 21% increase over 2025, up from our prior guidance of $2.25 to $2.29."

Oct 26, 2026: ExlService Holdings iMerit, an EXL Company,launches iMerit Scholars platform as AI talent war shifts to domain expertise

New platform connects domain experts with mission-critical AI work as companies compete for the human expertise needed to move AI from innovation to execution

NEW YORK, Oct. 26, 2026 -- iMerit, an EXL company, today announced the launch of its iMerit Scholars platform, designed to seamlessly manage hundreds of thousands of global domain experts (Scholars) who work onfine-tuning, evaluation and validation of generative AI models. This represents the first major launch since EXL (Nasdaq: EXLS) acquired iMerit in July to accelerate the progression of frontier AI into regulated enterprises.

The launch comes as the AI talent war heats up across generative and physical AI. The iMerit Scholars program already brings together a curated global community of 30,000 domain experts and linguists to support advanced model training, evaluation, validation and safety across specialized fields. The new iMerit Scholars platform provides a unified system to recruit, onboard, curate, assign, manage, engage, pay, and retain these experts at scale. It is powered by iMerit's proprietary Ango Hub, which allows customers and experts to collaborate on complex multimodal data to generate highly curated and validated training artifacts for high-stakes models.

"iMerit has always differentiated itself by a curated, people-first approach. We're seeing that become even more important as we scale up to help the frontier labs," said Radha Basu, executive vice president, head of iMerit at EXL. "The next phase of the GenAI race is about creating a painless and supportive infrastructure so that these highly capable specialists can enjoy their work and give their best. Scaling high quality and high engagement is mainly a human capital opportunity, backed by enabling technology. The iMerit Scholars platform is designed to create a seamless experience for top-notch talent to continue building and improving the next generation of AI."

"Now, as a part of EXL, we are expanding from the creation of models in frontier labs to the implementation of model workflows in highly regulated enterprises in banking, healthcare and insurance.

Press Release article 5 of 16, Source: West Corporation, 298 words

Oct 09, 2026: ExlService Holdings EXL transforms client workflows with the integration of agentic AI into core insurance and healthcare solutions

New agentic AI capabilities compress life and annuities insurance product launch cycles, optimize in-force policy management, and move health plan payment integrity upstream

NEW YORK, Oct. 08, 2026 -- EXL [NASDAQ: EXLS], a global data and AI company, announced it has integrated agentic AI across core solutions for insurance and healthcare, combining workflow orchestration, enterprise data intelligence and AI-powered automation to modernize operations and deliver better experiences for its clients. As part of its portfolio of integrated AI-led solutions, EXL is embedding AI agents directly into the offerings where its clients already work. This includes reimagining two of EXL's industry-leading solutions, EXL LifePRO and EXL Payment Integrity, with AI-powered automation to help modernize operations and deliver better outcomes.

EXL LifePRO Agentic Suite addresses a central challenge confronting the life insurance industry: slow product launch cycles. Most life insurers still need eight to ten months to bring a new product to market, a constraint that limits how many products a carrier can launch each year and how quickly it can respond to regulatory or competitive changes. According to a 2025 LIMRA and Bain & Company study, approximately 100 million Americans say they need life insurance but haven't purchased it, yet just 9.4 million policies were sold in 2024. Carriers are largely competing for the same buyers, leaving a large gap in demand unaddressed.

By automating manual, repetitive workflows, EXL LifePRO Agentic Suite compresses the product launch cycle to as few as 16 weeks, leveraging AI agents to ingest product specifications, interpret rules and rates, and propose executable configurations so teams can focus on reviewing and approval rather than build from scratch.

Oct 08, 2026: EXL transforms client workflows with the integration of agentic AI into core insurance and healthcare solutions

EXL [NASDAQ: EXLS], a global data and AI company, announced it has integrated agentic AI across core solutions for insurance and healthcare, combining workflow orchestration, enterprise data intelligence and AI-powered automation to modernize operations and deliver better experiences for its clients. As part of its portfolio of integrated AI-led solutions, EXL is embedding AI agents directly into the offerings where its clients already work. This includes reimagining two of EXLs industry-leading solutions, EXL LifePRO and EXL Payment Integrity, with AI-powered automation to help modernize operations and deliver better outcomes.

EXL LifePRO Agentic Suite addresses a central challenge confronting the life insurance industry: slow product launch cycles. Most life insurers still need eight to ten months to bring a new product to market, a constraint that limits how many products a carrier can launch each year and how quickly it can respond to regulatory or competitive changes. According to a 2025 LIMRA and Bain & Company study, approximately 100 million Americans say they need life insurance but havent purchased it, yet just 9.4 million policies were sold in 2024. Carriers are largely competing for the same buyers, leaving a large gap in demand unaddressed.

By automating manual, repetitive workflows, EXL LifePRO Agentic Suite compresses the product launch cycle to as few as 16 weeks, leveraging AI agents to ingest product specifications, interpret rules and rates, and propose executable configurations so teams can focus on reviewing and approval rather than build from scratch. The suite also makes conversions and implementation faster and easier, increasing straight-through processing across the board, with gains ranging from 18-60%, while built-in validation runs scenarios across coverage amounts, channels and jurisdictions, with full audit trails from actuarial, risk, and compliance sign-off.

Aug 18, 2026: EXL closes new $1 billion senior secured credit facility

Exhibit 99.1

EXL closes new $1 billion senior secured credit facility

NEW YORK Aug. 18, 2026 EXL (NASDAQ: EXLS), a global data and AI company, announced the closing of a new credit facility with PNC Bank, N.A., as Administrative Agent, and a syndicate of lenders that allows for borrowings of up to $1 billion. Bank of America, N.A., JPMorgan Chase Bank, N.A., and TD Bank, N.A. acted as joint lead arrangers on this transaction.

The facility increases EXLs borrowing capacity from the previous limit of $600 million and provides greater covenant flexibility to support the companys business strategy. The five-year senior secured credit agreement includes a $400 million term loan, permits revolver borrowings of up to $600 million, and has an accordion feature that would allow the facility to expand equal to the greater of $470 million or 100% of EBITDA for the trailing four quarters. The term of the new agreement will expire on August 18, 2031.

This deal reflects the confidence our banking partners have in EXLs financial strength and the long-term trajectory of our business, said Maurizio Nicolelli, chief financial officer of EXL. We have consistently prioritized a strong balance sheet, and this expanded debt capacity gives us the flexibility to extend our competitive advantage through targeted mergers and acquisitions while continuing to return capital to our shareholders under our $500 million share repurchase authorization. The closing of this facility is a clear demonstration of the focused execution against our capital allocation strategy.

In P&C insurance, ISG recognized EXL's strengths in data-first agentic AI orchestration, predictive underwriting analytics and outcome-based digital operations that help carriers transform data into real-time risk insights and decision-making. In L&R insurance, ISG cited EXL's intelligent operations, digital customer engagement capabilities and proprietary AI assets that support scalable, end-to-end policy administration and servicing.

"We are honored to be named a Leader in this ISG Provider Lens report for the fourth consecutive year, as it demonstrates our consistent commitment to helping our clients find new, innovative ways to achieve better decisioning, processes and business outcomes," said Vivek Jetley, president and head of insurance, healthcare and life sciences at EXL. "As the insurance industry continues to evolve, we are eager for the opportunity to hypercharge our clients' processes and keep them ahead of the curve."

"EXL treats P&C operations as a data problem as much as a process one," said Ashish Jhajharia, lead analyst at ISG. "By embedding analytics and AI directly into claims and underwriting workflows, it turns everyday transactions into sharper decisions with loss insight, faster triage, earlier fraud signals.

Ajay Ayyappan, executive vice president and general counsel at EXL, stated, 'At EXL, sustainability is not a side initiative; it is central to how we build a stronger, more resilient business.' EXLService Holdings, Inc. (NASDAQ:EXLS) has unveiled its new brand, ‘Go Beyond,’ which encapsulates the company’s commitment to innovation and transformation in the realms of data and artificial intelligence. This announcement comes as EXL celebrates its 20th year as a publicly traded entity, with the brand launch taking place on 10th September 2026 at a Nasdaq opening bell ceremony. The new branding aims to reflect EXL’s unique position at the intersection of data, AI, industry context, and enterprise execution, driving measurable outcomes for its clients.

Rohit Kapoor, chairman and chief executive officer of EXL, emphasized that ‘Go Beyond’ is not just a tagline but a daily standard for the company. He stated, "Our clients come to us to fundamentally reimagine how their enterprises operate. This strategic shift aims to further solidify EXL’s standing as a leader in leveraging data and AI for business transformation.

The rebranding initiative aligns with EXL’s foundational purpose of finding better ways to serve its clients and symbolizes its ongoing growth and adaptability. This includes the recent acquisition of iMerit in August 2026, which has expanded its capabilities in advanced AI model training and frontier AI development. Today, over 60% of EXL’s revenues are derived from data and AI-driven services, showcasing its market-leading growth trajectory.

Shirley Macbeth, chief marketing officer of EXL, remarked, "For 27 years, EXL has been built on a simple idea: We find a better way. ‘Go Beyond’ is the brand that captures that. EXL continues to serve a diverse clientele that includes leading corporations across various sectors such as insurance, healthcare, banking, and capital markets. August 20 2026: ExlService Holdings Expands Credit Capacity: A New Era for AI Investments?

ExlService Holdings, Inc. has taken a significant step in its financial strategy by entering into a new US$1.00 billion senior secured credit agreement with a syndicate led by PNC Bank, as announced on August 18, 2026. This move replaces its previous Citibank facility and extends revolving and term loan maturities to August 18, 2031.

The new credit facility is designed to provide ExlService greater balance sheet flexibility, allowing the company to fund acquisitions, technology investments, and share repurchases. It features leverage-linked pricing and covenants that impose defined limits on debt and interest coverage, which enhances the company's ability to pursue its ambitious goals in the AI sector.

As ExlService Holdings pivots from traditional outsourcing to a focus on data and AI, the new US$1.00 billion credit line appears to support the ongoing narrative of AI-driven growth. This expanded credit capacity is expected to bolster ExlService's strategy to secure AI-led deal wins while managing the associated risks, particularly those arising from rising wage inflation and compliance costs, which could complicate pricing power.

The recent acquisition of iMerit, which has brought its CEO into ExlService's executive team, further underscores the importance of scaling AI data services. Together with the larger credit facility, this acquisition highlights the company’s commitment to enhancing its capabilities in this high-demand sector, while also raising concerns related to execution risks in integrating new talent and maintaining resilient operating margins.

Analysts are optimistic about ExlService Holdings' growth potential, projecting revenues of approximately US$3.2 billion and earnings of around US$374.5 million by 2029. The new credit facility could reinforce this growth outlook; however, if cost pressures intensify more than anticipated, it may lead to a reassessment of the feasibility of meeting these targets.

August 18 2026: ExlService Holdings Inc Secures $1 Billion Financing Package with PNC Bank

ExlService Holdings, Inc. has entered into a significant financing agreement with PNC Bank, National Association, that includes a $600 million revolving credit facility and a $400 million term loan facility. The agreement, finalized on 18th August 2026, positions ExlService to enhance its liquidity and financial flexibility for future initiatives.

Under the new Credit Agreement, which matures on 18th August 2031, ExlService Holdings will benefit from a comprehensive $600 million revolving credit facility, which includes a $50 million swingline loan subfacility and a $20 million letter of credit subfacility. The term loan facility will also provide ExlService with additional capital, allowing it to strategically invest in growth opportunities while maintaining a strong balance sheet.

The terms of the Credit Agreement allow ExlService Holdings to adjust borrowing rates based on its Total Net Leverage Ratio, with interest rates ranging from 1.00% to 1.75% per annum depending on the chosen benchmark. This flexibility is designed to align with the company’s financial performance, ensuring cost-effective access to capital as needed.

As part of this financial restructuring, ExlService Holdings has used proceeds from the new credit facilities to fully repay its previous obligations under an agreement with Citibank, which included a $500 million revolving credit facility and a $100 million term loan facility. This transition was executed without incurring early termination penalties, allowing ExlService to streamline its debt obligations.

The new financing arrangement reflects ExlService Holdings' commitment to maintaining strong financial health, supported by a robust interest coverage ratio requirement of at least 3.00 to 1.00. The company is also permitted to increase its borrowing capacity under certain conditions, which could further enhance its operational capabilities and strategic growth initiatives in the coming years.

Oct 30, 2026: Blackstone Expands Indian Operations with New Base in Bengaluru

Blackstone has officially opened a new Global Capability Center in Bengaluru, marking its second operational base in India. This strategic move comes as part of the alternative asset manager's ongoing commitment to enhance support for its worldwide funds. The facility will primarily focus on operational execution and investment-support services while the front-office teams will continue to operate from the firm's existing Mumbai headquarters.

The Bengaluru center, established on 30th October 2026, will be led by Susan Yung, a senior managing director in Blackstone Credit & Insurance’s investment operations division. Ms. Yung brings over 25 years of financial sector experience and has previously worked in Bengaluru during her tenure at Goldman Sachs. This new location will house in-house staff and professional services partnerships aimed at improving the overall efficiency of Blackstone’s global operations.

By setting up operations in Bengaluru, Blackstone aligns itself with a growing trend among institutional investors to establish in-house execution platforms for functions like fund accounting, data compliance, and risk analytics. This strategy not only helps in securing captive tenancy within proprietary business parks—such as the jointly-owned Prestige Lakeshore Drive—but also reduces reliance on external business process outsourcing, thereby cutting operational costs.

Over the past two decades, Blackstone has cultivated a significant presence in the Indian market, emerging as the largest owner of office, retail, and logistics real estate in the country. The firm has successfully taken 14 Indian portfolio companies public and played a crucial role in launching five of the six listed real estate investment trusts (REITs) in India. This expansion into Bengaluru underscores Blackstone's long-term commitment to the region and its ambition to deepen its engagement with Indian capital markets.

As the company moves forward, market observers will closely monitor hiring trends at the new Bengaluru center and any potential transfer of further investment-support functions from Blackstone's offices in Europe and North America. Additionally, upcoming regulatory filings related to new Indian REIT listings and asset roll-ups in the retail and warehousing sectors will be pivotal in shaping Blackstone's future strategies in the region.

Oct 28, 2026: Blackstone Inc. Launches $1.1 Billion Senior Notes Offering

NEW YORK - Blackstone Inc. (NYSE:BX) priced an offering of $1.1 billion in senior notes through its indirect subsidiary Blackstone Reg Finance Co. L.L.C., according to a press release statement. The transaction was disclosed on 28th October 2026, highlighting the firm's ongoing strategy to utilize debt financing to bolster its capital structure.

The offering consists of $600 million of 5.650% senior notes due 2031 and $500 million of 5.900% senior notes due 2033. These senior notes will be guaranteed by Blackstone Inc. along with five of its indirect subsidiaries: Blackstone Holdings I L.P., Blackstone Holdings AI L.P., Blackstone Holdings II L.P., Blackstone Holdings III L.P., and Blackstone Holdings IV L.P. This guarantees the debt, providing investors with an added layer of security.

Blackstone stated that it intends to use the proceeds from this notes offering for general corporate purposes, a move that is expected to enhance its operational flexibility. The company has been proactive in managing its financing needs, and this latest offering is in line with its strategic objectives to maintain liquidity and support growth initiatives.

The notes were offered through an effective shelf registration statement filed with the U.S. Securities and Exchange Commission. The underwriting team for this offering includes notable financial institutions such as Citigroup Global Markets Inc., Goldman Sachs & Co. LLC, Morgan Stanley & Co. LLC, SMBC Nikko Securities America, Inc., and Wells Fargo Securities, LLC, underscoring the confidence in Blackstone's financial health.

As Blackstone Inc. continues to navigate the complexities of the financial markets, this offering reflects its commitment to maintaining a robust capital base while strategically positioning itself for future opportunities. Investors are likely to keep a close watch on how these funds will be deployed in the coming months.

Oct 27, 2026: Blackstone Leads $63B AI Compute Financing with Major Banking Partners

In a landmark transaction aimed at bolstering artificial intelligence capabilities, Bank of America, Blackstone, Citi, and Morgan Stanley have come together to arrange a substantial $63 billion financing package. This funding is specifically designed to support the deployment of AI compute capacity leveraging Broadcom’s cutting-edge technology. The announcement was made on 28th October 2026, with the financing expected to play a pivotal role in facilitating Anthropic's advanced AI initiatives.

The financing will be executed through a specially formed special-purpose vehicle (SPV), which has been established to manage the acquisition of Broadcom's XPU AI accelerators and networking solutions. The newly formed SPV will lease these technologies to Anthropic, marking a significant step in the company's journey towards enhancing its AI capabilities. Deliveries of the technology are anticipated to commence in calendar 2027, aligning with Anthropic's strategic goals.

Bank of America, Citi, and Morgan Stanley are taking the lead in arranging the first-lien portion of the financing, while Blackstone is stepping in as the sole provider of the second-lien financing. This collaborative effort underscores the financial community's confidence in the potential of AI technologies and the long-term vision shared by the involved parties. Blackstone also plays a crucial role by providing residual value support to the SPV, equivalent to the amount of the first-lien financing.

Jon Gray, president and COO of Blackstone, commented on the transaction, emphasizing the rapid evolution of AI technologies and the necessity for robust compute capacity to meet growing demands. The partnership with major banking institutions not only highlights Blackstone's commitment to advancing AI infrastructure but also reflects a broader trend in the financial sector towards investing in technological advancements that promise substantial returns.

As the landscape of artificial intelligence continues to evolve, this $63 billion financing arrangement positions Blackstone and its partners at the forefront of a transformative industry. The collaboration between these financial giants and Broadcom is expected to yield significant benefits for Anthropic, paving the way for innovative developments in AI that could reshape various sectors.

Oct 20, 2026: Blackstone Inc. Secures $8.7 Billion for New Energy Transition Fund

In a significant move for sustainable investing, Blackstone Inc. has successfully closed its fifth Blackstone Energy Transition Partners fund at a hard cap of $8.7 billion. This amount is over 50% larger than the previous fund, which raised $5.6 billion in 2025. The closing date of this fundraising effort is noted as 21st October 2026, with commitments primarily coming from state and city pension funds across Texas, Arizona, Michigan, Hawaii, Minnesota, and San Francisco.

David Foley, a key executive at Blackstone, emphasized the current landscape as a 'generational opportunity for experienced investors in the energy transition.' He highlighted the pressing challenges posed by the increasing demand for electricity, which has created a favorable environment for large funds like Blackstone to deploy substantial capital. The firm’s energy transition funds have reported a net internal rate of return (IRR) of 34% since their inception in 1997, underscoring their strong performance in the market.

The latest fund is poised to invest in various sectors, focusing on increasing manufacturing capacity, training skilled labor, and constructing critical energy infrastructure assets. Foley mentioned specific investments, including the recent acquisition of Flow Control Holdings, a Cincinnati-based company that produces components for liquid cooling systems used in AI data centers. Other notable portfolio companies include Dresser Utility Solutions, which provides infrastructure repair products for water and gas utilities, and Canada-based DarkVision, known for its ultrasound imaging technology used in assessing damages in wells and pipelines.

The influx of capital from public pensions into Blackstone's new fund signals a robust interest in impact investing, particularly in the energy sector. This trend is further evidenced by TPG's recent closure of its second Rise Climate fund at $10 billion earlier this fall. As the demand for clean energy solutions continues to rise, Blackstone’s strategic focus on energy transition aligns with broader market trends, positioning the firm to play a pivotal role in addressing the global shift toward sustainable energy.

As investors increasingly prioritize environmental, social, and governance (ESG) criteria, Blackstone Inc.’s substantial fundraising efforts reflect a growing commitment to sustainable practices. The firm’s ability to leverage its extensive network and expertise in private equity will likely enhance its influence within the energy transition space, making it a key player as the world seeks innovative solutions to meet its energy needs.

Oct 20, 2026: Blackstone Secures Approval for Essendi Acquisition in Chile

Blackstone Inc. has received regulatory approval for its acquisition of hotel operator Essendi from French hospitality group Accor. This decision, made by Chile's Fiscalia Nacional Economica (FNE), was announced on 21st October 2026 and confirms that the deal does not significantly threaten competition in the market.

The acquisition involves the transfer of control over Essendi's portfolio in Chile, which includes well-known hotels such as Novotel Vitacura, Mercure Santiago Centro, and five Ibis properties located in cities including Concepcion, Antofagasta, Providencia, Manquehue Norte, and Valparaiso. Notably, these hotels will continue to operate under the Accor brand through long-term franchise agreements, ensuring brand continuity for customers.

Essendi operates a range of hotels focused on the economy and mid-scale segments, primarily under the Ibis Budget brand, and has historical ties to Accor's operational structure. Blackstone, recognized as the world's largest alternative asset manager, is leveraging this acquisition to enhance its real estate investments, particularly in the hospitality sector.

The FNE's investigation began on 23rd September 2026 after the initial notification of the acquisition, submitted on 15th July, was deemed incomplete on two occasions. Ultimately, the regulator determined that there was no horizontal overlap in the Chilean market, as neither Blackstone nor its partner Colony, through the EarlyBird SCA vehicle, currently operates hotels in the region, thus mitigating potential vertical risks.

This acquisition aligns with Accor's strategy to adopt an 'asset-light' model, allowing the company to divest physical real estate while retaining commercial control through franchising arrangements. The clearance by Chilean authorities, alongside prior approval from the European Commission, underscores a significant trend in the hospitality industry, where hotel assets are increasingly transitioning to global real estate investors, separating property ownership from brand operations.

Oct 19, 2026: Blackstone Secures $8.7 Billion for Energy Transition Fund, Exceeding Expectations

NEW YORK-- Blackstone (NYSE: BX) today announced the final close of Blackstone Energy Transition Partners V ("BETP V") at $8.7 billion – more than 50% larger than its predecessor vehicle. The significantly oversubscribed fund reached its hard cap and builds on the strong performance of the strategy’s prior vintages. This announcement was made on 20th October 2026.

Blackstone Energy Transition Partners ("BETP") aims to develop energy companies that provide cleaner, more reliable, and affordable energy solutions to meet the growing global demand. The fund's energy transition investments have yielded a 34% net internal rate of return (IRR) since 1997, underscoring the effectiveness of its distinct strategy. In 2025, BETP received the prestigious title of IJ Investor’s "Fund Manager of the Year – Equity" for North America, reflecting its leadership in delivering significant investment performance.

David Foley, Global Head of Blackstone Energy Transition Partners, emphasized the generational opportunity for investors in the energy transition sector. He stated, "By backing exceptional management teams with flexible, patient equity capital, we are increasing manufacturing capacity, enhancing skilled labor recruitment, and constructing critical energy infrastructure assets. BETP is focused on delivering solutions that not only address the challenges posed by rising electricity demand but also build companies to scale, create jobs, and generate lasting value for our investors."

BETP V commenced its investment period in June and has already funded three platform companies—Kindle, Fisterra, and Viridon—while also making three acquisitions targeting energy efficiency and resiliency. Since January 2025, BETP has committed over $11 billion in new equity investments across the energy transition ecosystem, including significant transactions in energy equipment manufacturing and essential services.

Blackstone Energy Transition Partners is known for its control-oriented equity investments in energy-related businesses, boasting a long-term track record with over $34 billion of equity committed globally across various energy sectors. The strategy strategically avoids investments in hydrocarbon reserves and extraction, focusing instead on solutions that promote cleaner, more affordable energy. As the world’s largest alternative asset manager, Blackstone continues to seek compelling returns for its investors while actively contributing to the growth of sustainable energy solutions.

10.11 Satisfies four criteria of Benjamin Graham

- The P/E of 26.3 is 0.3 times the highest average P/E of 79.4 in the last five years. This is a value criterion, according to Benjamin Graham who described as a value criterion "A P/E ratio down to less than four-tenth of the highest average P/E ratio the stock attained in the most recent five years".

-"Current ratio of two or more"; current assets are 14.5 times current liabilities.

-"Stability of growth in earnings over the last 5 years, defined as no more than two declines of 5% or greater in year-end earnings"; there have been 2 declines in earnings in the last 5 years.

10.12 Satisfies 5 out of 9 criteria of Joseph Piotroski [pass mark 5]:

- Positive net income.

- Positive operating cashflow.

- Good quality of earnings [operating cashflow exceeds net income].

- Improvement in current ratio from 13.5 to 14.5.

- Improvement in asset turnover [growth in revenue of 22.1% exceeded growth in assets of 9.8%].

But does not meet the following 4 criteria of Joseph Piotroski:

- Return on Assets improvement.

- Improvement in long-term debt to total assets.

- Total shares on issue unchanged (or reduction in total shares on issue).

- Improvement in gross margin.

SECTION 11 CORPORATE PROFILE

11.1 Activities

Blackstone Inc., a global leader in alternative asset management and listed on the New York Stock Exchange, specializes in private equity, real estate, credit, and hedge fund investments. Founded in 1985, the firm manages a diverse portfolio of assets for institutional and individual investors, focusing on sourcing, executing, and overseeing investments that deliver long-term value across sectors like infrastructure and growth equity. Its core activities include raising capital, making strategic investments, and providing advisory services to enhance portfolio performance. Among its fastest-growing segments are private credit and real estate, fueled by rising demand for alternative financing solutions and urbanization trends, respectively. This expansion has positioned Blackstone as a key player in global markets, with a strong track record of innovation and operational excellence. It is the 3rd largest Asset manager company by market capitalization among NYSE- and TXSE-listed stocks.

The benchmark S&P/ASX 200 is moving well above the 8,900 level, with gains in energy and financial stocks partially offset by weakness in mining and technology stocks.

The benchmark S&P/ASX 200 Index is gaining 42.90 points or 0.48 percent to 8,934.70, after touching a high of 8,992.70 earlier. The broader All Ordinaries Index is up 34.40 points or 0.37 percent to 9,046.00. Australian stocks closed notably higher on Thursday.

Among the big four banks, National Australia Bank, Commonwealth Bank and ANZ Banking are gaining more than 1 percent each, while Westpac is adding almost 1 percent.

Gold miners are mostly lower. Genesis Minerals is down more than 1 percent, Evolution Mining is edging down 0.3 percent, Resolute Mining is declining more than 4 percent and Newmont is losing almost 1 percent, while Northern Star Resources is gaining more than 1 percent.

Shares in Reece are jumping almost 10 percent following the release of its first-quarter sales revenue update.

In economic news, Australia's final demand producer price index increased 1.1 percent quarter-on-quarter in the third quarter of 2026, slightly beating market forecasts for a 1.0 percent rise but slowing from a 1.3 percent increase in the previous period, which was the strongest price growth since the third quarter of 2023. On an annual basis, the PPI increased 3.6 percent, unchanged from the previous quarter.

Australia's private sector credit rose by 0.6 percent on month in September 2026, slightly exceeding market expectations for a 0.5 percent increase but matching the pace recorded in the previous two months. On an annual basis, private sector credit growth held steady at 8.4 percent, unchanged from August.

In the currency market, the Aussie dollar is trading at $0.703 on Friday.

The Japanese market is trading sharply lower on Friday, snapping a three-session winning streak, following the broadly negative cues from Wall Street overnight. The Nikkei 225 is tumbling 2.4 percent to below the 63,050 level, with weakness in index heavyweights, financial and technology stocks.

The benchmark Nikkei 225 Index closed the morning session at 63,019.78, down 1,639.69 points or 2.54 percent, after hitting a low of 62,711.42 earlier. Japanese shares ended significantly higher on Thursday.

Market heavyweight SoftBank Group is tumbling more than 6 percent, while Uniqlo operator Fast Retailing is gaining more than 2 percent. Among automakers, Toyota is edging down 0.3 percent, while Honda is edging up 0.3 percent.

In the tech space, Advantest is tumbling almost 9 percent and Tokyo Electron is slipping almost 5 percent, while Screen Holdings is plunging almost 8 percent.

In the banking sector, Sumitomo Mitsui Financial and Mitsubishi UFJ Financial are losing more than 1 percent each, while Mizuho Financial is declining almost 3 percent.

Among the major exporters, Mitsubishi Electric and Panasonic are losing more than 4 percent each, while Sony is gaining almost 2 percent and Canon is edging up 0.3 percent.

Tokyo Electric Power, Taiyo Yuden, TDK and Kokusai Electric are slipping more than 7 percent each. Chubu Electric Power, Resonac Holdings and Sumco are declining almost 7 percent each, while Mitsui Kinzoku is losing more than 6 percent.

Conversely, Konami Group is soaring more than 18 percent and Oriental Land is jumping more than 7 percent, while ANA Holdings, Capcom and BANDAI NAMCO are surging more than 6 percent each. Nomura Research Institute is advancing almost 6 percent, while Shiseido and Nitori Holdings are advancing almost 5 percent each. Nippon Yusen K.K. and Ryohin Keikaku are adding more than 4 percent each. Dentsu Group, Japan Airlines, NEXON, BayCurrent and Toho are up more than 3 percent each.

In economic news, the Bank of Japan will wrap up its monetary policy meeting on Friday and then announce its decision on interest rates. The BoJ is expected to keep its benchmark lending rate unchanged at 1.25 percent.

Retail sales in Japan advanced 2.3 percent on year in September 2026, below market expectations for a 3.3 percent rise and easing from an upwardly revised 2.9 percent increase in the previous month, marking the slowest growth in three months. It was the seventh consecutive month of growth in retail sales. On a monthly basis, retail sales dropped 0.7 percent in September, following a revised 0.9 percent fall in August, marking the second consecutive monthly decline.

Japan's unemployment rate edged down to 2.4 percent in September 2026, below market expectations and August's figure of 2.5 percent. The number of unemployed fell by 100 thousand to 1.67 million, while employment increased by 340 thousand to a four-month high of 68.68 million. Meanwhile, the jobs-to-applicants ratio rose to 1.19 from 1.18 in each of the previous three months, exceeding consensus of 1.18.

Core consumer prices in Tokyo's central wards increased 2.6 percent on year in October 2026, following a 2.7 percent rise in September and in line with expectations. It was the first deceleration in five months, following the fastest increase since November 2025 in September.

Japan's industrial production increased by 2.2 percent month-over-month in September 2026, reversing a 2.4 percent decline in the previous month and surpassing market estimates of 1.3 percent, preliminary data showed. It was the fastest growth since January. On an annual basis, production rose 1.9 percent, down from 2.7 percent in August, marking the softest growth in four months.

In the currency market, the U.S. dollar is trading in the lower 157 yen-range on Friday.

Elsewhere in Asia, South Korea and Taiwan are tumbling 5.4 and 3.2 percent, respectively. Indonesia is down 1.8 percent, while China, Singapore and Malaysia are lower by between 0.2 and 0.8 percent each. New Zealand and Hong Kong are up 0.2 and 1.2 percent, respectively.

Meanwhile, the major European markets all moved to the upside on the day. While the U.K.'s FTSE 100 Index rose by 0.3 percent, the French CAC 40 Index shot up by 1.1 percent and the German DAX Index surged by 1.3 percent.

Crude oil prices moved lower on Thursday on expectations of a possible de-escalation in the U.S.-Iran conflict as Qatar presses Iran to negotiate with the U.S. West Texas Intermediate crude for December delivery was down $0.79 or 0.77 percent at $101.99 per barrel.

SECTION 1 Appen Corporate profile

Activities

Appen Limited is a global provider of high-quality data solutions for artificial intelligence (AI) and machine learning, operating as an Australian company with shares traded as American Depositary Receipts (ADRs) in the U.S. The company's core activities involve sourcing, annotating, and labeling data to train AI models, serving clients across industries such as technology, automotive, and healthcare. Appen specializes in human-in-the-loop processes for tasks like image recognition, speech processing, and natural language understanding, ensuring accurate and scalable datasets that enhance AI performance. Among its fastest-growing segments are services for natural language processing and computer vision, propelled by surging demand for advanced AI applications in autonomous systems and digital assistants. This expansion underscores Appen's role in supporting the global AI ecosystem, with a focus on ethical data practices and technological innovation to drive client success. It is the International Depository Receipt (IDR) market's 21st largest Electronics company by market capitalization.

UNITED STATES EDITION

30 October 2026 08:45 EDT

Appen Ltd. (ASX:APX) finds itself in the spotlight as artificial intelligence spending continues to evolve, particularly following Anthropic's recent listing filing. As of the 28th of October 2026, the Australian data services provider has become increasingly relevant in discussions surrounding the high-quality data that AI developers require to train their models effectively.

Appen supplies crucial data services, including collection, labelling, and evaluation, leveraging a vast network of human contributors. This human-checked data is essential for model developers, who rely on it to train systems, assess accuracy, and identify weaknesses before public release. Unlike technology licensors, Appen's revenue model hinges on customer outsourcing decisions and the margins it can achieve in the process.

The recent attention on Anthropic's filing highlights the heavy financial commitments needed for advanced AI model development, signaling a sustained demand for quality data services. While this may bode well for Appen, it also indicates that major players in the AI space may increasingly build in-house capabilities, which could pressure pricing for data services. Moreover, Meta's fund-raising efforts to support its AI initiatives suggest that even the most resource-rich companies are looking externally to bolster their data supply chains.

Appen's share price has seen significant fluctuations this month, reflecting broader market sentiment rather than any specific company announcements. This volatility underscores how small-cap technology stocks like Appen can react sharply to shifts in AI sentiment, particularly when compared to larger players in the sector. Commentary around the company's performance often draws comparisons to other ASX-listed firms, positioning Appen as a unique case study in the AI supply chain.

Looking forward, market observers will closely monitor Appen's efforts to diversify its customer base. The company has historically relied on a small number of large clients, which poses risks if one decides to alter its strategy. As automation encroaches on simpler labelling tasks, the demand for more complex evaluation work—where human judgement remains essential—may provide Appen with new opportunities, should it successfully navigate these competitive waters.

Traders seemingly remained reluctant to make significant moves ahead of the US Federal Reserve's interest rate decision later in the day, followed by rate decisions from the European Central Bank and Bank of Japan later this week. All three central banks are expected to keep the key rate unchanged.

CME Group's FedWatch Tool is currently indicating a 92.8 percent chance the Fed will leave rates unchanged tomorrow but a 78.0 percent chance of a quarter point rate hike in December.

Australian shares are trading modestly higher on Wednesday, extending the gains in the previous three sessions, with the benchmark S&P/ASX 200 moving above the 8,850 level, following the broadly positive cues from Wall Street overnight, with gains across most sectors led by mining and technology stocks.

The markets pared early gains following the release of hotter-than-expected domestic inflation data, which boosted expectations for a fifth rate hike this year.

The benchmark S&P/ASX 200 Index is gaining 18.00 points or 0.20 percent to 8,868.10, after touching a high of 8,937.00 earlier. The broader All Ordinaries Index is up 22.80 points or 0.25 percent to 8,991.60. Australian stocks ended notably higher on Tuesday.

Among major miners, BHP Group and Rio Tinto are gaining almost 1 percent each, while Mineral Resources is advancing more than 3 percent and Fortescue is adding more than 1 percent.

Oil stocks are mostly higher. Woodside Energy and Santos are advancing more than 2 percent each, while Beach energy is gaining almost 1 percent. Origin Energy is edging down 0.4 percent.

Among gold miners, Evolution Mining is advancing almost 3 percent, Resolute Mining is rising almost 4 percent, Genesis Minerals is surging more than 4 percent, Newmont is adding almost 1 percent and Northern Star Resources is gaining more than 1 percent.

In economic news, Australia's annual inflation rate accelerated to 4.3 percent in September 2026 from 4.0 percent in August, above market expectations of 4.2 percent, marking the highest rate since March. The reading also moved further above the central bank's 2 to 3 percent target range.

The trimmed mean CPI rose to 3.6 percent on year from 3.5 percent, in line with forecasts, while the weighted median eased to 3.4 percent from 3.6 percent.

On a monthly basis, the CPI increased by 0.8 percent, exceeding expectations of a 0.7 percent rise and accelerating from a 0.4 percent gain in August to mark its largest increase in six months.

In the currency market, the Aussie dollar is trading at $0.703 on Wednesday.

The Japanese stock market is trading sharply higher on Wednesday, extending the gains in the previous session, following the broadly positive cues from Wall Street overnight. The Nikkei 225 is moving above the 64,050 level, with gains in index heavyweights and technology stocks.

The benchmark Nikkei 225 Index closed the morning session at 64,067.10, up 798.73 points or 1.26 percent, after touching a high of 64,260.86 earlier. Japanese stocks ended sharply higher on Tuesday.

Market heavyweight SoftBank Group is gaining more than 2 percent and Uniqlo operator Fast Retailing is adding almost 2 percent. Among automakers, Honda is edging up 0.1 percent, while Toyota is flat.

In the tech space, Advantest and Screen Holdings are gaining almost 3 percent each, while Tokyo Electron is advancing more than 3 percent.

In the banking sector, Sumitomo Mitsui Financial is losing almost 1 percent and Mitsubishi UFJ Financial is edging down 0.3 percent, while Mizuho Financial is gaining almost 1 percent.

Among the major exporters, Mitsubishi Electric is edging up 0.3 percent and Panasonic is gaining almost 1 percent, while Canon is declining almost 6 percent and Sony is losing more than 1 percent.

Among other major gainers, Kioxia Holdings is soaring more than 8 percent and Taiyo Yuden is surging almost 8 percent, while Renesas Electronics and Sumco are advancing more than 5 percent each. Ibiden and TDK are gaining more than 4 percent each, while Murata Manufacturing, Kyocera, Disco, JX Advanced Metals, SMC and Shin-Etsu Chemical are adding almost 4 percent each. Socionext and Denka are up more than 3 percent each.

Conversely, Capcom is tumbling almost 11 percent, Nitto Denko is slipping more than 5 percent and Konami Group is declining almost 5 percent, while Asahi Group and Kirin Holdings are losing more than 3 percent each. Tokio Marine and BANDAI NAMCO are down almost 3 percent each.

In the currency market, the U.S. dollar is trading in the higher 157 yen-range on Wednesday.

Elsewhere in Asia, South Korea and Taiwan are surging 5.8 and 1.2 percent, respectively. New Zealand and China are up 0.3 percent each. Singapore, Malaysia and Indonesia are lower by between 0.1 and 0.8 percenteach. Hong Kong is relatively flat.

On Wall Street, stocks showed another notable move to the upside during trading on Tuesday after starting the week off on an upbeat note. The tech-heavy Nasdaq led the way higher, bouncing further off the two-month closing low set last Friday.

The Nasdaq gave back some ground going into the end of the day but still jumped 308.43 points or 1.2 percent to 26,477.60. The S&P 500 also climbed 45.83 points or 0.6 percent to 7,722.60, while the narrower Dow posted a more modest gain, inching up 15.99 points or less than a tenth of a percent to 52,377.57.

Meanwhile, the major European markets also ended the day mixed. While the U.K.'s FTSE 100 Index slid by 0.8 percent, the French CAC 40 Index climbed by 0.5 percent and the German DAX Index advanced by 0.7 percent.

Crude oil prices slumped again on Tuesday on efforts by regional intermediaries in the gulf to defuse Middle East tensions. West Texas Intermediate crude for December delivery sank $1.73 or 1.70 percent at $100.03 per barrel.

### 19. [After Firmus IPO flop, here are five Australian AI stocks to watch](https://ct.moreover.com/?a=60721019498&p=21l&v=1&x=FtcoXco3IE9WfzSNj9F_JA)

- **Matched Innodata Pillars:** Infrastructure Engineering
- **Matched Competitors:** Appen, TELUS Digital, Sama, TaskUs, CloudFactory, iMerit
- **Coverage Snippet:** Link to Image

Megaport (ASX: MP1)

Megaport is a Network as a Service (NaaS) provider that principally provides on-demand interconnection services. With a market cap of A$3.8 billion, it's one of the largest players in the local sector and the largest Software Defined Network (SDN) platform globally. It has more than 4,000 customers.

It logged FY26 revenue of A$312 million, up from A$227.06 million in FY25, with EBITDA up 24% to A$77.1 million. Its stock price has gained around 31% since the start of the year, while CEO Michael Reid told shareholders in August that it's seen increased business scale, broadening markets and larger opportunities.

For investors, the attraction is Megaport's ability to benefit from growing demand for cloud and AI connectivity without having to build data centres itself. However, with a market capitalisation equivalent to roughly 12 times annual revenue, expectations are already high.

Artrya (ASX: AYA)

Artrya is a medical tech company developing and commercialising a patented AI platform for detection and diagnosis of coronary disease. Its solution claims to offer rapid assessment of chest pain patients in emergency and primary care settings.

With a market cap of A$402 million, it established a commercial revenue base in FY26, securing contracted revenue across three foundation US customers: Tanner Health, Cone Health, and Northeast Georgia Health System. Its balance sheet of A$74m cash is set to support further US growth plans.

The investment case rests on whether Artrya can turn these initial US contracts into recurring revenue and expand adoption across the healthcare sector.

BrainChip (ASX: BRN)

BrainChip is an AI on-chip processing and learning company that develops software and hardware for advanced artificial intelligence. According to the company, it's the world's first commercial producer of neuromorphic AI tech.

Commercial adoption and revenue scale remain key questions, although the company reported revenue of $1.88 million for FY25, a 374% lift from the year before. However, its stock price has slipped around 38% since January.

The 374% revenue increase looks impressive, but the low starting base puts that growth into perspective. BrainChip remains a speculative investment, with its prospects depending on its ability to demonstrate demand for its technology.

Appen (ASX: APX)

Appen provides data services for deep learning and generative AI systems. Some 80% of leading global Large Language Model (LLM) builders are customers, it says.

It reported H1 FY26 revenue of A$119.9 million, up 17% from the prior corresponding period. Also positive, net loss was $4.45 million compared to $19.26 million a year ago. The company's stock price is up around 46% since January.

The narrowing loss suggests Appen's turnaround is gaining traction, although a return to sustainable profitability remains to be demonstrated.

NEXTDC (ASX: NXT)

NEXTDC, with a market cap of A$4.2 billion, is Australia's largest data centre developer. It also develops and operates data centres in New Zealand, Malaysia and Japan.

Some gloss came off in October after it slashed its revenue forecast and paused future builds, saying about A$140 million worth of contracted customer revenue this financial year would be deferred. Still, it reported a 16% lift in revenue for FY26 to A$405 million, with EBITDA up 15% to A$248.8 million.

The revenue deferral raises questions about the timing of returns on NEXTDC's infrastructure investments. Data centres require substantial upfront capital, so delays in customer revenue can put pressure on cash flow and financing needs even when longer-term demand remains strong. Will the postponed revenue materialises as expected?

Market headwinds

CMC Invest market analyst Henry Fisher said NEXTDC and Megaport offered infrastructure exposure, while BrainChip, Appen and Artrya were more specialised AI businesses.

He cautioned that "higher interest rate expectations, volatile bond yields and questions around AI valuations are making the market less forgiving".

"There's also the risk of infrastructure spending running ahead of demand, while smaller AI specialists face technology advancing so quickly that their products lose value before they've properly established themselves."

"If the US AI boom unwinds badly, the fallout could reach well beyond tech stocks, hitting Australian banks through financial markets and miners through weaker global growth," he added.

Wed Sep 30, 2026: ExlService Reports Significant Sustainability Advances in Latest Report

ExlService Holdings, Inc. [NASDAQ: EXLS] has published its seventh annual Sustainability Report, titled 'Embedding Sustainability,' showcasing significant advancements in corporate social responsibility (CSR), emissions reductions, and responsible AI governance. The report, released on 30th September 2026, highlights EXL's commitment to integrating sustainability into its core business practices as a means to enhance long-term value for stakeholders.

In the report, dated 2026-10-01 16:41:39.309490, EXL reveals a remarkable 64% reduction in Scope 1 and 2 greenhouse gas emissions from its 2019 baseline. This achievement is complemented by an impressive 78% of the global workforce participating in various CSR initiatives, reflecting the company's dedication to fostering a culture of community engagement and environmental stewardship.

Ajay Ayyappan, executive vice president and general counsel at EXL, stated, 'At EXL, sustainability is not a side initiative; it is central to how we build a stronger, more resilient business.' He emphasized that the report embodies the company's ethos of continuous improvement, demonstrating measurable progress across all facets of its sustainability agenda, including a robust increase in community investment and the maturation of its Responsible and Secure AI program.

The 2025 Sustainability Report adheres to the Global Reporting Initiative Standards and the Sustainability Accounting Standards Board Software and IT Services Standard, ensuring transparency and accountability. Additionally, it aligns with the recommendations of the Task Force on Climate-related Financial Disclosures and maps to the United Nations Sustainable Development Goals, further solidifying EXL's commitment to global sustainability efforts.

EXL remains focused on propelling enterprises to enhance their business models through data and AI, with an emphasis on sustainable practices. As the company continues to innovate and lead in responsible corporate governance, the insights from this report may be of particular interest to investors looking to align their portfolios with companies demonstrating strong environmental, social, and governance (ESG) practices.

Wed Sep 9, 2026: EXL Launches ‘Go Beyond’ Brand, Emphasizing Data-Driven Transformation

EXLService Holdings, Inc. (NASDAQ:EXLS) has unveiled its new brand, ‘Go Beyond,' which encapsulates the company's commitment to innovation and transformation in the realms of data and artificial intelligence. This announcement comes as EXL celebrates its 20th year as a publicly traded entity, with the brand launch taking place on 10th September 2026 at a Nasdaq opening bell ceremony. The new branding aims to reflect EXL's unique position at the intersection of data, AI, industry context, and enterprise execution, driving measurable outcomes for its clients.

Rohit Kapoor, chairman and chief executive officer of EXL, emphasized that ‘Go Beyond' is not just a tagline but a daily standard for the company. He stated, "Our clients come to us to fundamentally reimagine how their enterprises operate. With AI transforming every industry, we've built something rare; a company with domain depth to know what questions to ask and data and AI capabilities to answer them at scale." This strategic shift aims to further solidify EXL's standing as a leader in leveraging data and AI for business transformation.

The rebranding initiative aligns with EXL's foundational purpose of finding better ways to serve its clients and symbolizes its ongoing growth and adaptability. Since its foray into data and analytics in 2006, EXL has consistently pushed the boundaries of traditional industry norms. This includes the recent acquisition of iMerit in August 2026, which has expanded its capabilities in advanced AI model training and frontier AI development. Today, over 60% of EXL's revenues are derived from data and AI-driven services, showcasing its market-leading growth trajectory.

Shirley Macbeth, chief marketing officer of EXL, remarked, "For 27 years, EXL has been built on a simple idea: We find a better way. ‘Go Beyond' is the brand that captures that. It's an invitation to our clients and employees to push the boundaries of what's possible and a reflection of the company EXL has become, a global data and AI leader that combines deep domain and context expertise with trusted execution to drive outcomes that matter." This statement underscores EXL's focus on fostering an innovative culture that prioritizes client success.

EXL continues to serve a diverse clientele that includes leading corporations across various sectors such as insurance, healthcare, banking, and capital markets. As the company embarks on this new branding journey, stakeholders will be keenly observing how ‘Go Beyond' translates into tangible outcomes and strategic advantages for clients in an increasingly competitive landscape. For more insights into EXL's transformation and the ‘Go Beyond' brand in action, interested parties can visit the company's official website.

Thu Aug 20, 2026: ExlService Holdings Expands Credit Capacity: A New Era for AI Investments?

ExlService Holdings, Inc. has taken a significant step in its financial strategy by entering into a new US$1.00 billion senior secured credit agreement with a syndicate led by PNC Bank, as announced on August 18, 2026. This move replaces its previous Citibank facility and extends revolving and term loan maturities to August 18, 2031.

The new credit facility is designed to provide ExlService greater balance sheet flexibility, allowing the company to fund acquisitions, technology investments, and share repurchases. It features leverage-linked pricing and covenants that impose defined limits on debt and interest coverage, which enhances the company's ability to pursue its ambitious goals in the AI sector.

As ExlService Holdings pivots from traditional outsourcing to a focus on data and AI, the new US$1.00 billion credit line appears to support the ongoing narrative of AI-driven growth. This expanded credit capacity is expected to bolster ExlService's strategy to secure AI-led deal wins while managing the associated risks, particularly those arising from rising wage inflation and compliance costs, which could complicate pricing power.

The recent acquisition of iMerit, which has brought its CEO into ExlService's executive team, further underscores the importance of scaling AI data services. Together with the larger credit facility, this acquisition highlights the company's commitment to enhancing its capabilities in this high-demand sector, while also raising concerns related to execution risks in integrating new talent and maintaining resilient operating margins.

Analysts are optimistic about ExlService Holdings' growth potential, projecting revenues of approximately US$3.2 billion and earnings of around US$374.5 million by 2029. The new credit facility could reinforce this growth outlook; however, if cost pressures intensify more than anticipated, it may lead to a reassessment of the feasibility of meeting these targets.

It is the NASDAQ's 6th largest Business services company by market capitalization.

Wed Sep 9, 2026: EXL Launches ‘Go Beyond’ Brand, Emphasizing Data-Driven Transformation

EXLService Holdings, Inc. (NASDAQ:EXLS) has unveiled its new brand, ‘Go Beyond,' which encapsulates the company's commitment to innovation and transformation in the realms of data and artificial intelligence. This announcement comes as EXL celebrates its 20th year as a publicly traded entity, with the brand launch taking place on 10th September 2026 at a Nasdaq opening bell ceremony. The new branding aims to reflect EXL's unique position at the intersection of data, AI, industry context, and enterprise execution, driving measurable outcomes for its clients.

Rohit Kapoor, chairman and chief executive officer of EXL, emphasized that ‘Go Beyond' is not just a tagline but a daily standard for the company. He stated, "Our clients come to us to fundamentally reimagine how their enterprises operate. With AI transforming every industry, we've built something rare; a company with domain depth to know what questions to ask and data and AI capabilities to answer them at scale." This strategic shift aims to further solidify EXL's standing as a leader in leveraging data and AI for business transformation.

The rebranding initiative aligns with EXL's foundational purpose of finding better ways to serve its clients and symbolizes its ongoing growth and adaptability. Since its foray into data and analytics in 2006, EXL has consistently pushed the boundaries of traditional industry norms. This includes the recent acquisition of iMerit in August 2026, which has expanded its capabilities in advanced AI model training and frontier AI development. Today, over 60% of EXL's revenues are derived from data and AI-driven services, showcasing its market-leading growth trajectory.

Shirley Macbeth, chief marketing officer of EXL, remarked, "For 27 years, EXL has been built on a simple idea: We find a better way. ‘Go Beyond' is the brand that captures that. It's an invitation to our clients and employees to push the boundaries of what's possible and a reflection of the company EXL has become, a global data and AI leader that combines deep domain and context expertise with trusted execution to drive outcomes that matter." This statement underscores EXL's focus on fostering an innovative culture that prioritizes client success.

EXL continues to serve a diverse clientele that includes leading corporations across various sectors such as insurance, healthcare, banking, and capital markets. As the company embarks on this new branding journey, stakeholders will be keenly observing how ‘Go Beyond' translates into tangible outcomes and strategic advantages for clients in an increasingly competitive landscape. For more insights into EXL's transformation and the ‘Go Beyond' brand in action, interested parties can visit the company's official website.
